检查原因与下一步

  • 已保留的原始候选问题：论点的引用类型不满足所选判断范围。记录了某人的说法，不等于证实了所述事件。

    原论点：The supplied records lack in-trip verbal call audio, so absence of verbal notification cannot be definitively proven from the chat log alone, though no verbal notice was acknowledged by the rider.

本次结果

退款金额：未裁定 SGD · 页面只展示建议，不执行收费或退款。

  • Reported road-route deviation is 4.3 km, which exceeds the mandatory 2.0 km autonomous threshold under Section 6.2, legally requiring human review.
  • Lack of independent historical traffic API provenance for traffic records TC-001 and TC-002 under Section 1.4.
  • Absence of contemporaneous trip call audio recordings to determine whether verbal route deviation notification or rider agreement occurred per Section 1.2 and Section 1.4.
  • Both advocates agree that Section 6.2 requires human review because the excess route distance of 4.3 km exceeds 2.0 km.
  • No safety indicators were flagged in this case.

已记录的依据

  • 暂无可采纳的裁决依据。

计算结果与待澄清问题

算术结果与已明确的规则分开列出；未明示的收费条件仍需澄清。距离／差额不等于责任／退款。

  • route_comparison采样路线 20746.2 m / 参考 19896.0 mE-001 E-003 E-004 E-005 E-006 E-007 E-008 E-009 E-010 E-011 E-012 E-013 E-014 E-015 E-016 E-017 E-018 E-028 E-030 E-031 E-040
  • reported_route_distance报告道路距离 24.8 km / 参考 20.5 km / 差额 4.3 kmE-035 E-040 E-044
  • fare_difference6.50 SGD 差额（未裁定退款）E-037 E-038 E-045
  • reported_excess_distance_fare原记录金额 3.44 SGD（未裁定退款）E-027
  • 需 Judge 分析 · ROUTE_JUSTIFICATION_REQUIRES_REVIEW

    Distance and fare arithmetic do not evaluate rider approval, independent traffic or safety evidence, or whether an extra charge was justified. Review the supplied context and missing information.

  • 必须补充政策或数据 · TRAFFIC_API_PROVENANCE_MISSING

    Traffic records are supplied by the sample. Historical traffic API provider/response provenance required by section 1.4 is not supplied.

  • 必须补充政策或数据 · VERBAL_ROUTE_NOTICE_RECORD_MISSING

    The supplied chat is available; no trip call recording is supplied to check a verbal route notification or rider acknowledgment. Do not infer that no verbal notice occurred.

  • 政策要求人工裁决 · ROUTE_DISTANCE_REQUIRES_HUMAN_REVIEW

    Section 6.2 requires human review when reported route deviation exceeds 2 km, regardless of a possible refund rationale.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2

Judge 如何看双方观点

以下为通过结构和引用检查的分析；这些检查不能证明自然语言推理正确。

核心问题

Whether the driver's route deviation of 4.3 km and resulting fare increase can be autonomously resolved under Policy v2.1 or must be escalated to human review due to the 2.0 km route deviation threshold.

乘客观点 · 部分有依据支持

The rider side correctly notes the reported 4.3 km excess road distance and identifies that Section 6.2 mandates human review for deviations over 2 km, though the claim of lack of verbal notice remains unverified due to missing call records.

涉及论点：opening-rider-claims-0 opening-rider-claims-1 opening-rider-claims-2 opening-rider-claims-3 opening-rider-claims-4 opening-rider-counterpoints-0 opening-rider-counterpoints-1 response-rider-claims-0 response-rider-claims-1 response-rider-claims-2 response-rider-claims-3 response-rider-claims-4 response-rider-counterpoints-0 response-rider-counterpoints-1 response-rider-counterpoints-2

依据：E-002 E-023 E-024 E-027 E-034 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 E-040 E-041 E-042 E-044 E-045 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1

司机观点 · 部分有依据支持

The driver side accurately documents severe traffic incidents on the PIE and trip duration, and concurs that Section 6.2 mandates escalation to human review for deviations exceeding 2.0 km.

涉及论点：opening-driver-claims-0 opening-driver-claims-1 opening-driver-claims-2 opening-driver-claims-3 opening-driver-counterpoints-0 opening-driver-counterpoints-1 response-driver-claims-0 response-driver-claims-1 response-driver-claims-2 response-driver-claims-3 response-driver-counterpoints-0 response-driver-counterpoints-1

依据：E-002 E-021 E-023 E-024 E-027 E-031 E-032 E-033 E-036 E-039 E-040 E-041 E-042 E-043 E-044 E-045 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1

逐条论点评估

  • opening-rider-claims-0 缺少支持证据

    Original assertion reports that the driver took an unnotified alternate route causing 4.3 km extra distance; E-002 proves the attribution of the complaint, but does not independently establish absence of verbal notice throughout the trip.

    支持记录：无 · 反驳记录：无

    政策：P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1

    待补充：Contemporaneous call audio or in-vehicle verification of communication.

裁决关键条件

  • route_justification · 满足

    Supplied traffic incident records TC-001 and TC-002 document a vehicle fire and severe congestion on the eastbound PIE during the trip window.

    E-040 E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • distance_tolerance · 不满足

    Reported road route distance is 24.8 km vs 20.5 km recommended, creating a 4.3 km excess distance that exceeds the 1.0 km policy tolerance.

    E-035 E-040 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • timely_route_notice · 未知

    In-app chat contains no prior route notice, but call recordings are missing, leaving verbal notification under Section 1.2 unverified.

    E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • rider_acknowledgment · 未知

    No in-app acknowledgment is recorded, and verbal communication cannot be verified without trip audio recordings.

    E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • extra_charge · 未知

    While Section 1.3 outlines refund rules, the dispute exceeds the Section 6.2 2.0 km autonomous threshold, barring agent determination.

    E-027 E-035 E-040 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2

争议点分析

  • Mandatory human review gate under Section 6.2 for route deviations over 2 km

    Section 6.2 sets a strict hard limit that route deviation cases can only be decided autonomously if deviation is 2 km or less. The reported excess distance is 4.3 km (24.8 km minus 20.5 km), which triggers mandatory escalation.

    E-035 E-040 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2
  • Substantiation of traffic justification and verbal route notification

    Traffic conditions TC-001 and TC-002 show congestion on the PIE, but external API provenance is unauthenticated under Section 1.4, and the lack of in-trip audio recordings prevents determining whether verbal notice was provided under Section 1.2.

    E-023 E-024 E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4

复核问题逐项处理

  • ROUTE_JUSTIFICATION_REQUIRES_REVIEW · 仍待澄清

    Traffic incident records exist in the sample, but verification of actual rider notification and charging entitlement requires human review in light of the excess distance gate.

    E-023 E-024 E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • TRAFFIC_API_PROVENANCE_MISSING · 仍待澄清

    The case records provide traffic data, but historical traffic API provenance is not independently certified per Section 1.4.

    E-041 E-042 E-043 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • VERBAL_ROUTE_NOTICE_RECORD_MISSING · 仍待澄清

    No call recordings are present in the record to verify or disprove verbal route deviation notification or rider acknowledgment under Section 1.2 and Section 1.4.

    E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • ROUTE_DISTANCE_REQUIRES_HUMAN_REVIEW · 仍待澄清

    The reported road route deviation is 4.3 km, exceeding the 2.0 km threshold under Section 6.2 and mandating human escalation.

    E-035 E-040 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2

仍需补充的信息

  • Official historical traffic API authentication records verifying incidents TC-001 and TC-002 per Section 1.4.
  • Trip call audio recordings or contemporaneous in-vehicle communication logs to establish whether verbal notice was given or agreed to per Section 1.2.
  • Human reviewer adjudication of the route deviation exceeding 2.0 km pursuant to Section 6.2.

先例对照

  • P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1

    相似点：Both cases involve a driver rerouting due to traffic congestion on the recommended path, with no prior in-app notification given to the rider.

    差异：In precedent DISP-2026-0089, the route deviation was 1.8 km (allowing an autonomous ruling under Section 6.2), whereas here the deviation is 4.3 km, exceeding the 2.0 km threshold and requiring mandatory human escalation.

本次绑定的政策与程序检查

RYDE-POLICY-V2.1 · 生效 2026-01-01

人工审核门槛

  • ROUTE_DISTANCE_REQUIRES_HUMAN_REVIEW Section 6.2 requires human review when reported route deviation exceeds 2 km, regardless of a possible refund rationale. P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2

逐项政策条件

  • route_justification · met

    Supplied historical incident records overlap the recommended route/time; they are not independently authenticated API responses.

    E-028 E-030 E-031 E-040 E-041 E-042 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • distance_tolerance · not_met

    Use reported road-route kilometres; at most one excess kilometre is within the listed tolerance.

    E-035 E-040 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • timely_route_notice · unknown

    Recorded driver notice contains route change/reason and is no later than the observed deviation (or before trip start). Missing call recordings do not prove no verbal notice.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • rider_acknowledgment · unknown

    Explicit contextual rider acknowledgment can establish agreement; generic thanks or missing logs cannot.

    P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • extra_charge · unknown

    Route tolerance/justification and notification must be assessed separately from charging entitlement.

    E-035 E-040 E-041 E-042 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4

程序保守修正

程序保留原输出，对不满足来源要求的论点降低支持评级，或按逐条评价修正汇总标签，然后重新检查全部规则。Fare 仅在政策条件及主依据独立成立时允许此修正；裁决与金额不变，未执行任何收费或退款。

双方陈述与回应

offline_placeholder 只摘录已有陈述，不代表已完成真实 Agent 辩论。

乘客 Advocate

第一轮陈述

  • The rider stated that the driver took an unnotified alternate route via Geylang and East Coast area, resulting in 4.3 km of extra distance and an overcharge. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1
  • The reported trip distance was 24.8 km compared to the recommended direct route of 20.5 km, generating an excess distance of 4.3 km and an excess fare of $6.50. E-034 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 E-040 E-044 E-045 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • In-app chat records show that when the rider questioned the route at 13:24:00, the driver provided a generic reassurance without giving a traffic reason or prior notice before deviating. E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • Because the excess route distance of 4.3 km exceeds the 2 km threshold, Section 6.2 mandates escalation to human review rather than an automated agent ruling. E-036 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2
  • Under Section 1.3, if traffic justification is recognized but the rider was not informed per Section 1.2, the rider is entitled to a partial refund of the excess distance fare ($3.44), or a full refund of excess distance fare if traffic justification is rejected. E-027 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1
  • Organizer-supplied traffic incident records report a vehicle fire and heavy congestion on the PIE eastbound during the trip window, which could provide traffic justification for rerouting. E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • The supplied records lack in-trip verbal call audio, so absence of verbal notification cannot be definitively proven from the chat log alone, though no verbal notice was acknowledged by the rider. E-002 E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • Independent historical traffic API authentication records to formally substantiate the PIE incident under Section 1.4.
  • Trip call recording or in-vehicle audio logs to confirm whether any verbal notification of deviation was given to and acknowledged by the rider.
  • Human reviewer determination regarding the excess route distance exceeding 2 km pursuant to Section 6.2.

回应对方

  • The rider stated that the driver took an unnotified alternate route via Geylang and East Coast area, resulting in 4.3 km of excess distance and an overcharge. E-002 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1
  • Reported road distance records show the trip covered 24.8 km compared to the recommended 20.5 km route, producing an excess distance of 4.3 km (well beyond the 1.0 km tolerance) and a total fare difference of $6.50. E-027 E-034 E-035 E-036 E-037 E-038 E-039 E-040 E-044 E-045 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • Section 1.2 requires the driver to inform the rider before or at the deviation point with a brief reason; however, the in-app chat shows the driver provided only generic reassurance at 13:25:00 after the rider inquired at 13:24:00, with no advance notice or traffic explanation recorded. E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • Because the reported route deviation of 4.3 km exceeds the 2.0 km threshold defined in Section 6.2, an automated resolution cannot be executed and mandatory escalation to human review is required. E-036 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2
  • Under Section 1.3, if the reviewer determines the reroute was justified by traffic but unnotified, the rider is entitled to a partial refund of the excess distance fare ($3.44), whereas if traffic justification is rejected due to unauthenticated records, the full excess distance fare ($3.44) is refundable. E-027 E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1
  • While the driver advocate argues the reroute was justified by severe traffic on the PIE corridor, the supplied traffic records are organizer-provided samples lacking independent historical traffic API authentication as required by Section 1.4. E-041 E-042 E-043 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • Even if the human reviewer accepts the traffic justification and notes the trip duration was 37 minutes, traffic justification alone does not eliminate the Section 1.2 notification requirement nor exempt the driver from excess distance fare refund rules under Section 1.3. E-032 E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3
  • The driver side correctly notes that missing in-trip call recordings do not disprove verbal notice; however, the supplied records contain no proof of verbal disclosure or rider agreement. E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • Independent historical traffic API provenance or authenticated source verification for incidents TC-001 and TC-002 per Section 1.4.
  • Trip call audio recordings or contemporaneous communication records to evaluate whether verbal notice and rider acknowledgment occurred per Section 1.2 and Section 1.4.

司机 Advocate

第一轮陈述

  • The driver's rerouting was justified by active, severe traffic incidents on the recommended PIE corridor during the trip window. E-040 E-041 E-042 E-043 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • The driver completed the trip promptly in 37 minutes, compared to the estimated 35 minutes, successfully avoiding the severe congestion on the PIE to deliver the rider on time for their scheduled flight. E-021 E-031 E-032 E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1
  • Under policy section 1.3, even when a driver reroutes for traffic without recorded prior notification, the rider is only eligible for a partial refund of the excess distance fare ($3.44), not a refund of the entire fare difference ($6.50) or trip. E-027 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1
  • Because the reported route deviation of 4.3 km exceeds the 2.0 km threshold, Section 6.2 mandates escalation to human review, precluding an automated resolution. E-036 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2
  • The rider stated that the driver did not inform them of any route change or traffic, and the in-app chat does not record an advance traffic explanation prior to rerouting. E-002 E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • The supplied records do not include an in-trip call recording to verify whether verbal notice or agreement occurred as permitted under section 1.2. E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • Trip call audio recording or in-person verbal communication verification to determine whether verbal notice of the reroute was given at or before the deviation point per Section 1.2.
  • Official historical traffic API authentication records to independently verify the provenance of incidents TC-001 and TC-002 per Section 1.4.

回应对方

  • The driver's deviation from the PIE was justified under Section 1.1 by documented severe traffic conditions, specifically an active vehicle fire blocking lanes 1 and 2 on the recommended PIE eastbound corridor during the trip window. E-040 E-041 E-042 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.4
  • The alternate route successfully avoided substantial congestion and delivered the rider to Changi Airport T3 in 37 minutes, just 2 minutes over the estimated 35-minute duration, ensuring timely arrival for the rider's 15:30 flight. E-021 E-031 E-032 E-033 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.1
  • Because the reported route deviation of 4.3 km exceeds the 2 km threshold specified in Section 6.2, mandatory policy rules require escalating this dispute to human review rather than executing an automated agent refund. E-036 E-044 P-RYDE-2.1-S6.2
  • Under Section 1.3, where traffic justification exists, lack of written chat notification limits any potential rider remedy to a partial refund of the excess distance fare ($3.44), precluding the rider's demand for a full excess fare refund of $6.50 or any trip refund. E-027 E-039 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3 P-RYDE-2.1-S7.1
  • While the rider asserts no route change notification was provided and in-app chat does not record an advance traffic explanation, Section 1.2 permits verbal notification, and the absence of trip call or in-cabin audio recordings means verbal communication cannot be conclusively proven or disproven. E-002 E-023 E-024 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.2
  • The rider's reliance on a $6.50 overcharge claim conflates the total fare difference with the excess distance fare component, which is recorded as only $3.44 alongside $2.22 in excess tolls. E-027 E-039 E-045 P-RYDE-2.1-S1.3
  • Independent historical traffic API provenance or authenticated source logs to formally verify the PIE incident under Section 1.4.
  • Trip call audio recordings or in-vehicle communication records to determine whether verbal notice and acknowledgment occurred pursuant to Section 1.2 and Section 1.4.
  • Human reviewer determination regarding the excess route deviation exceeding 2 km pursuant to Section 6.2.
